Dear  Sir/Madam,  
 
With reference to our letter dated July 17 , 2025 (Ref No.: RML/2025- 26/592)  informing that the 21st Annual 
General Meeting of Route Mobile Limited (“ the Company ”) is scheduled to be held on Friday , September 12, 
2025  at 3:30 P.M. (IST)  through Video Conferencing  (“VC”) / Other Audio -Visual  Means (‘OAVM”) , in 
accordance with relevant circulars issued by the  Ministry of Corporate Affairs and the Securities and Exch ange 
Board of India (‘SEBI’).  
 
Pursuant  to Regulatio n 34(1)  of SEBI  (Listing  Obligations  and Disclosure  Requirements)  Regulations,  2015 
(“Listing Regulations”) , please find enclosed  herewith  the Annual  Report  of the Company  along  with  Notice  
convening  the 21st Annual  General  Meeting  and other statutory reports for the Financial Year 2024- 25, which 
is being sent through electronic mode to all those shareholders  of the Company whose email ids are registered 
with the Company and/or Depository Participant(s). Further, in accordance with the Regulation 36(1)(b) of 
the Listing Regulations, the Company has initiated sending a letter to the shareholders whose e -mail ad dresses 
are not registered with the Company/RTA/DPs, providing a web -link from where the Annual Report can be 
accessed on the website of the Company.  
 
The Notice of AGM along with the Annual Report for the Financial Year 2024- 25 is also available on the 
Company's website at https://routemobile.com/compliance/2025/Annual -Report -2025.pdf . Our 
Sustainability Report  for the Financial Year 2024- 25 can also be accessed at  
https://routemobile.com/compliance/2025/Sustainability -Report -FY-2024 -25.pdf .  
 
We request you to kindly take the above information on record for the purpose of diss emination to the  
shareholders.  
 
Thanking  you,  
 
Yours  truly,  
For Route  Mobile  Limited  
  
 
 
Tejas Shah

Excellence
Building on its legacy as a CPaaS leader, Route Mobile is entering a new 
era of growth as part of Proximus Global, seamlessly integrating the 
strengths of BICS and Telesign. This powerful alliance extends our ability 
to drive engagement across the entire digital communications spectrum, 
securing trust with advanced digital identity solutions and delivering 
hyper-personalised, omnichannel experiences at scale.
With deep operator connectivity, enhanced security and intelligent 
identity capabilities, we are redefining how businesses connect, protect 
and engage with customers worldwide. Guided by a future-focused vision, 
Route Mobile is poised to shape the next chapter of the CPaaS landscape 
by uniting operator access, identity intelligence and omnichannel 
innovation, helping brands thrive in a digital-first, hyper-connected world.FY 2024-25 Highlights

Advancing Digital 
Identity for Trust and 
ComplianceDriving Omnichannel 
and Hyper-Personalised 
EngagementMaximising 
Operator Access and 
Firewall ValueThe Proximus Global 
Advantage
As fraud becomes more sophisticated and 
regulatory frameworks grow more complex, 
digital identity is emerging as a key differentiator. 
Leveraging Telesign’s expertise, Route Mobile is 
enhancing its ability to deliver advanced identity 
verification, risk scoring and fraud detection 
tools. These capabilities empower businesses to 
safeguard users, build lasting trust and ensure 
regulatory alignment across every interaction.The modern customer journey is nonlinear, 
spanning multiple touchpoints and channels. 
Route Mobile is leading this shift by enabling 
seamless, hyper-personalised engagement 
through tools like WhatsApp Voice, eSIM 
integration and AI-driven interaction platforms. By 
meeting users on their preferred channels in real 
time, we help brands create frictionless, connected 
experiences that deepen customer relationships 
and drive lasting loyalty.Through BICS’ extensive operator network and 
infrastructure, Route Mobile gains direct access 
to global telecom ecosystems. This enables us to 
deploy intelligent firewall solutions that enhance 
traffic security, eliminate gray routes and unlock 
new messaging monetisation opportunities for 
operators. By expanding our operator footprint, 
we deliver greater network control, revenue 
assurance and value across the messaging chain.As part of Proximus Global, Route Mobile 
combines the strengths of BICS and Telesign to 
connect, protect and engage across the digital 
communications value chain. This integration 
unites expertise in cloud communications, digital 
identity and global connectivity - driving innovation 
through robust CPaaS platforms, advanced 
fraud prevention and API-first delivery. Backed 
by a global technology leader, we are expanding 
into key markets like the US and Europe while 
accelerating product diversification to deliver 
future-ready solutions for a hyper-connected world.

Future-proofing for Enduring Global Success  
Securing Long-term 
Global Leadership
In a rapidly evolving digital world, today’s investments define tomorrow’s 
leadership. At Route Mobile, we are laying strong foundations for 
enduring global success by building a secure, intelligent and omnichannel 
communications platform. Anchored in the synergies of Proximus Global - 
bringing together Route Mobile, BICS and Telesign - we are reshaping the 
future of digital engagement.
Harnessing the Power of Proximus Global
The creation of Proximus Global unlocks a unique value chain that seamlessly connects enterprise 
demand (Route Mobile), operator access (BICS) and digital trust (Telesign). Together, we deliver an 
integrated Connect–Protect–Engage framework, offering enterprises a unified solution with global 
coverage, trusted identity and compliance-by-design.
The Connect–Protect–Engage Advantage
Our Four Strategic PillarsCONNECT

and fraud prevention

Omnichannel customer

Route MobileReinforcing Digital Trust through  
Identity and Fraud Prevention
As cyber risks grow more complex, digital identity 
and fraud prevention are emerging as mission-
critical differentiators. Through Telesign’s advanced 
capabilities, Route Mobile now offers identity 
verification, risk scoring and compliance-ready 
fraud detection tools, enabling enterprises to 
safeguard user trust at scale. Our AI-powered 
365guard firewall proactively combats spam, 
phishing and artificially inflated traffic (AIT),  
setting new benchmarks in enterprise security.
90%
of digital fraud today stems from 
compromised user identity
Route Mobile is addressing this challenge by 
combining telecom-grade security with 
intelligent identity solutions.
India’s position as the world’s largest messaging 
hub and a leader in regulatory frameworks (like the 
DPDP Act) equips us with a proven playbook for 
fraud prevention and compliance—capabilities we 
are now scaling globally.

Future-proofing for Enduring Global Success  
Expanding Our Global Footprint
The integration with Proximus Group is a powerful 
catalyst for international expansion. We are:
 →Penetrating mature markets like Europe 
and North America with enhanced 
enterprise offerings
 →Deepening our presence in high-growth 
markets, including India, Africa and Asia-Pacific
 →Accelerating enterprise access through 
BICS’ operator network and Telesign’s 
identity ecosystem
 →By combining local expertise with global scale, 
we are positioning Route Mobile as the preferred 
CPaaS partner for enterprises worldwideBuilding the API Economy of the Future
The future of communications lies in 
programmable telecom networks—and we are 
already shaping this transformation.
Through initiatives like Catana and Aduna, Route 
Mobile is opening telco network APIs to enterprises, 
enabling capabilities such as:
 →Fraud prevention
 →Authentication and verification
 →Geolocation and consent management
 →Omnichannel orchestration
This API-first approach unlocks new revenue 
streams for operators while enabling developers 
to integrate telco-grade capabilities into 
enterprise workflows.
Omnichannel Intelligence for the  
Modern Enterprise
Today’s customer journey is non-linear, 
spanning SMS, WhatsApp, RCS, Email and Voice 
within a single interaction. With OCEAN, our 
omnichannel experience suite, we empower 
brands to orchestrate personalised, insight-driven 
campaigns, enabling real-time, context-aware 
engagement at scale.
Our solutions - ranging from WhatsApp, Voice to 
AI-driven transit ticketing on RCS - extend beyond 
messaging, creating frictionless, connected 
experiences that build deeper customer loyalty.

Overview of Proximus Global
The integration of Route Mobile into Proximus Global marks the union 
of two global pioneers, combining a legacy of over 60 years in digital 
communications. This strategic alliance creates a powerful ecosystem 
spanning the full Connect–Protect–Engage value chain, empowering 
enterprises with secure, scalable and intelligent communication 
solutions. By aligning Route Mobile’s CPaaS expertise, BICS’ global 
telecom infrastructure and Telesign’s digital identity and fraud 
prevention capabilities, we are redefining how businesses communicate, 
protect and build trust with their customers worldwide.

A leading global communications enabler, BICS is 
one of the world’s top voice carriers and a major 
provider of mobile data services. It enhances 
Route Mobile’s global reach by delivering extensive 
international carrier services, strengthening 
connectivity, performance and scale across the 
communications value chain.A fast-growing leader in authentication and digital 
identity services, Telesign secures communications 
for some of the world’s largest digital brands and 
cloud-native businesses. While digital identity 
remains its core, Telesign has significantly 
expanded its CPaaS offerings, complementing 
Route Mobile’s capabilities with more secure, 
trusted and intelligent communications.
Years of 
combined 
expertiseProximus Group is a leading provider 
of future-ready connectivity, IT and 
digital services in the Benelux region 
and global markets.
With a clear ambition to build Belgium’s #1 
gigabit network, Proximus is at the heart of 
creating inclusive digital ecosystems that 
deliver trustworthy, secure and sustainable 
digital experiences.

Enhanced Business Messaging, Voice, Email, SMS Filtering, Analytics

by Conversational AI.
From social media and banking to 
e-commerce, travel and beyond, we 
simplify how businesses connect,

Guillaume Antoine Boutin 
Chairman & Non-Executive Director
(Non-Independent) (Up to April 17, 2025)
Guillaume Antoine Boutin is the Chairman and Non-Executive 
Director of Route Mobile. He also serves as the Chief Executive 
Officer of Proximus, Chairman of the Board of Directors at 
BICS and Telesign, a member of the Proximus Art Board and 
CEO ad interim of BICS.
He holds a baccalauréat scientifique and pursued 
Telecommunications Engineering at Telecom Sud Paris, 
completing the Programme Grande École in 1997, followed 
by a Programme Grande École degree from HEC Paris 
in 1999.
Mr. Boutin began his career at a web start-up before joining 
SFR, where he held various roles in strategy, finance and 
marketing. In 2015, he became Chief Marketing Officer 
at Canal+ Group. In August 2017, he joined the Proximus 
Executive Committee as Chief Consumer Market Officer, 
later assuming his current leadership positions.
Mark James Reid 
Chairman & Non-Executive Director
(Non-Independent)*
Mark Reid is a Non-Executive, Non-Independent Director 
on the Route Mobile Board, as well as a Board Member of 
BICS and Proximus Global. Since May 2021, he has served 
as Group Financial Lead at Proximus, reporting directly to 
the Group CEO. He is also currently the ad interim CEO of 
Proximus Global S.A. Mr. Reid holds an Honours Degree in 
Aeronautical Engineering from the University of Glasgow and 
is a Chartered Accountant certified by the Chartered Institute 
of Management Accountants (CIMA).
With over 20 years of senior financial leadership experience 
in international telecom, digital media and travel companies, 
Mr. Reid has worked extensively in Switzerland, the UK and the 
US. Prior to joining Proximus, he was Chief Financial Officer for 
the Central European Region at Liberty Global, based in Zurich 
for five years. Before that, he served as Deputy CFO at Virgin 
Media in London, also part of the Liberty Global group. Earlier 
in his career, he held a range of senior financial positions 
across the telecom, digital media and travel industries.
*Appointed as a Chairman w.e.f April 17, 2025.
Rajdipkumar Gupta 
Managing Director & CEO*
Rajdipkumar Gupta serves as the Managing Director and 
Chief Executive Officer of Route Mobile Limited. As the 
founder of the Company, he has been the driving force 
behind its transformation into one of the fastest-growing 
technology and cloud communications companies globally. 
Widely recognised as one of the youngest tech entrepreneurs 
in the world, his strategic vision has positioned Route Mobile 
among the leading providers of Communications Platform as 
a Service (CPaaS) solutions.
Guided by his leadership, Route Mobile enables enterprises 
and brands to deliver seamless, end-to-end communication 
experiences through a broad portfolio of offerings — including 
Messaging, Voice, Firewall, Email and advanced messaging 
channels such as RCS, WhatsApp, Viber and IP messaging. 
The Company has established a strong global presence 
with over 16 offices across North America, LATAM, Europe, 
the Middle East, Africa and the Asia-Pacific region. He holds 
a Bachelor’s degree in Science (Physics) from the University 
of Mumbai and a Master’s Diploma in Software Engineering 
from Aptech Computer Education. With more than 
20 years of expertise in software design and development, 
he continues to lead Route Mobile with a spirit of innovation, 
adaptability and an unwavering focus on delivering value to 
customers worldwide.
*Re-designated as MD & CEO w.e.f. July 18, 2025.
Jan Van Acoleyen 
Non-Executive Director
(Non-Independent) (w.e.f. April 16, 2025)
Jan Van Acoleyen has served as the Human Capital Lead of 
the Proximus Group since May 2016. Proximus is a leading 
digital communications company with approximately 12,000 
employees, operating in the Benelux and international 
markets. It is the top provider of communication and ICT 
services in Belgium and ranks among the top three global 
providers of CPaaS, interconnection and digital identity 
services. Jan reports directly to the President & CEO of 
Proximus Group and is a member of its Executive Committee.
He also holds multiple board positions, serving as Chair of 
BeMobile and as a Board Member of Belgacom International 
Carrier Services, Telesign, Proximus Global, MWingz and the 
Proximus Pension Fund. In addition, he is an Independent 
Board Member of SD Worx and Vlaeynatie.Jan began his career in 1988 as a Consultant and Business 
Manager at DIP Consulting in Brussels, where he worked until 
1994. He then joined Alcatel, holding several HR leadership 
positions in its telecommunications and semiconductor divisions, 
ultimately serving as Vice President of HR for Alcatel Western 
Europe until 2004. From 2004 to 2006, he was Corporate Vice 
President of Human Resources at Agfa-Gevaert N.V., before joining 
Barco in January 2007 as Chief Human Resources Officer, a role 
he held until May 2016 when he moved to Proximus.
He earned a Master’s degree in Educational Policy & 
Management Sciences from KU Leuven in 1986, followed by an 
EMBA from the Antwerp Management School in 1991. He has 
also completed executive programmes in human resources 
and business at the London Business School and Stanford 
University, as well as the Directors’ Programme at INSEAD.
Passionate about working in fast-paced technology 
environments, Jan focuses on building and scaling high-
performing teams and organisations. He believes that culture, 
teamwork and organisational capabilities are decisive factors 
in achieving sustainable global growth.
Anil Kumar Chanana
Independent Director
Anil Chanana is a finance veteran with over 40 years of 
experience spanning global IT and analytics. He currently 
serves as a business consultant in strategy and finance and 
sits on the boards of both listed and privately held entities 
backed by private equity sponsors. His board engagements 
cover a diverse range of sectors, including CDMO, API, 
intermediates and formulations, sports and athleisure 
footwear, IT services, next-generation customer experience 
and health benefits administration.
Previously, Mr. Chanana served as the Chief Financial Officer 
of HCL Technologies Limited, where he worked closely with 
the board to formulate and execute strategic priorities.
A Chartered Accountant by profession, Mr. Chanana holds a 
B.Com (Honours) degree from the University of Delhi and has 
completed the Financial Management Programme at the 
Stanford Graduate School of Business.
Harita Gupta
Independent Director
Harita Gupta is an Independent Director on the Board of 
Route Mobile. She also serves as Head of Asia Pacific at 
Sutherland, where she leads the IT/ITES services business across four geographies—Philippines, India, China 
and Malaysia.
With over three decades of global experience in the digital 
and IT services sector, she has held leadership roles at 
Microsoft India and NIIT Technologies prior to joining 
Sutherland. Her expertise lies in driving digital transformation 
and innovation, enabling businesses to leverage emerging 
opportunities for growth.
Mrs. Gupta is the Chairperson of the BPM Council at 
NASSCOM and a member of NASSCOM’s Executive Council. 
She holds a Master’s degree from IIT Delhi and is recognised 
as a visionary leader with a strong focus on building 
interpersonal connections. A committed advocate for 
inclusivity, she champions workplace diversity, particularly for 
People with Disabilities, members of the LGBTQ+ community 
and other underrepresented groups.
She is also an influential voice in the industry on the 
transformative impact of Generative AI and Machine Learning 
on business models and the talent landscape.
Prakash Advani
Independent Director
Prakash Advani is a seasoned, multidisciplinary C-suite 
executive with over two decades of leadership experience 
in financial institutions across EMEA. Having secured 
fit-and-proper approvals from the European Central Bank 
and financial regulators in Belgium, the Netherlands, Italy, 
Germany and Luxembourg, he has held senior management 
roles in both banking and non-banking financial organisations 
in these markets.
In his executive career, Mr. Advani spearheaded multiple 
strategic transformations, serving as Country Executive for 
the Luxembourg and Belgium branches of the Royal Bank 
of Scotland (RBS) and later as Group Head of Strategy and 
Structuring at the French-Belgian bank Dexia Group. He has 
played pivotal roles in structuring and negotiating mergers 
and acquisitions, as well as leading funding and treasury 
operations across industries, including as Executive Director 
of Corporate Finance Belgium at ABN AMRO and Head of 
Corporate Coverage at RBS Belgium.
Currently, as a Non-Executive and Independent Director at 
Bank Nagelmackers—a Belgian retail and private bank—he 
chairs the Risk Committee and serves on the Audit and 
Nominations Committees. He also provides strategic advisory 
services to boards, family offices and controlling shareholders, 
supporting growth initiatives and critical decision-making. 
From 2016 to 2020, he served as an Expert Evaluator for the 
European Union’s Horizon 2020 Programme.
An alumnus of Harvard Business School and a Chartered 
Accountant, Mr. Advani is recognised for his collaborative 
leadership style, strategic insight and exceptional networking 
and team-building skills, with a proven track record across 
Europe, Asia and the Middle East.Board of Directors

Director – Finance Route Mobile (UK) Limited

and member of the Institute of Chartered

Accounting. As Route Mobile’ s promoter since 
inception, he brings over 21 years of expertise 
in audit, accounts, business analysis and SAP 
consulting. Before co-founding Route Mobile, he 
held senior positions at PricewaterhouseCoopers 
and Covansys (India) Private Limited.

Rajeshwar Singh Gill
Group Chief Financial Officer 
Raj Gill is a seasoned finance executive with

Product Portfolio
365guard
AI-powered SMS Spam and Fraud Protection Solution
Proximus Global
Proximus Global’s latest innovation, launched via its subsidiary 365squared, is 
365guard— an AI-driven solution designed to shield mobile network operators 
(MNOs) and users from SMS-based scams, fraud and smishing. It leverages 
real-time, adaptive analytics to detect and block suspicious activity across both 
P2P and A2P messaging channels.
By replacing static, rule-based systems with a dynamic AI engine that learns 
and evolves with emerging threats, 365guard logs suspicious activity, alerts 
operators and either blocks fraud or tags messages for users—fortifying global 
SMS networks and bolstering regulatory compliance.
This capability complements Route Mobile’s broader focus on trust and 
security—reinforcing Proximus Global’s unified approach to protecting digital 
communications at scale.Specifications
Features and Benefits
  Real-time SMS Monitoring and 
Advanced Content Filtering
  Instant spam/fraud detection using 
URL checks, keyword matching and 
message scoring
  Sender Reputation and Behavioural/
Anomaly Detection
  Detects risky senders like SIM boxes 
and monitors traffic/user feedback for 
suspicious patterns
  Flexible Actions and Easy Integration/
Scalability
  Allows blocking or alerting users; deploys 
quickly standalone or with SMS firewalls; 
scales with trafficDeployment
Simple, fast integration with MNO networks. 
Can operate as a standalone module 
or alongside 365secure SMS solution 
suite. Integrates seamlessly with existing 
SMS Firewalls.
Compatibility
Works with existing SMS gateways, firewalls 
and 365secure products.Scalability
Designed to scale with increasing traffic and 
business growth.
Compliance
Fully encrypted, secure and GDPR-compliant.
  AI & Machine Learning Powered with 
User-friendly Portal
  Adaptive spam detection using ML/
NLP; offers real-time alerts, analytics 
and reporting
  Compliance, Security and  
Cost Efficiency
  Encrypted, GDPR compliant; reduces 
network load and billing issues

  Expert management to reduce

Chairman ’s Message
Creating Connected 
Solutions through 
Innovation
It is a privilege to address you as Chairman of Route 
Mobile, commencing my tenure in April 2025. 
Reflecting back on the last 12 months with the 
Board and Management, Route Mobile and all the 
talented teams have worked tirelessly, making 
significant progress supporting our customers’ 
objectives on many fronts. Let me highlight just 
two of the many initiatives the team has achieved 
during the year 2024-2025.
 →Our Omnichannel focus at Route Mobile 
continues to be of paramount strategic 
importance. An example of Route Mobile’s 
thought leadership in this space is its expanded 
WhatsApp-based  metro ticketing projects for 
Mumbai Metro and PT MRT Jakarta’s integrated 
solution via vending machines and email. We launched advanced ticketing capabilities for 
L&T Metro Rail Hyderabad through RCS and 
Google Wallet services,  demonstrating our 
commitment to customer-centric solutions. 
We continue targeting high-potential verticals 
in financial services, retail, healthcare, 
travel, government and logistics, using our 
omnichannel platform to build recurring 
revenue streams while maintaining disciplined, 
profitable growth.
 →As part of Proximus Global, Route Mobile has 
directly benefited from strategic partnerships 
and alliances with Microsoft, Infosys and 
other partners to advance our leading CPaaS 
technologies and Identity solutions via joint go-
to-market initiatives. Additionally,  our partnership with Nokia focuses 
on the network API ecosystem, enabling us 
to build innovative applications and deliver 
transformative enterprise solutions across 
diverse market segments.
Now, as we look ahead, the Board and the 
Management team are fully focused on further 
accelerating the benefits that we can achieve by 
combining Route Mobile’s DNA with the scale and 
global reach of Proximus Global. BICS, Telesign and 
Route Mobile are now united and working together 
under one powerful ecosystem, benefiting from 
the leverage, technology and sheer scale of 
Proximus Global. An opportunity like this is not 
just structural, but strategic as well. By integrating 
global connectivity, digital identity and CPaaS 
capabilities, we are uniquely positioned to serve 
enterprises end-to-end across geographies.
Route Mobile is Poised to Capture Value 
Amid Shifting Industry Dynamics
While the CPaaS industry has faced headwinds 
in recent quarters, including loss of low-margin 
business and customer shifts to alternate 
channels, we remain optimistic about our 
growth opportunities.
CPaaS is moving rapidly into AI-powered, 
predictive and secure communications. We are 
investing decisively in R&D, automation and fraud 
prevention, such as 365guard, as well as next-
generation customer experience tools, including RCS, WhatsApp-based commerce and integrated 
payment-enabled messaging. We are committed 
to innovation across our platforms to serve 
enterprises globally through our superior product 
depth in CPaaS, APIs and Identity solutions.
Within the Proximus Global framework, we now 
have the capacity to accelerate product rollouts, 
penetrate new geographies at speed and engage 
in joint innovation programmes that redefine the 
role of CPaaS in the digital economy.
Our achievements are built on the trust and 
support of our stakeholders. We remain committed 
to transparent governance and long-term value 
creation for our shareholders and to delivering 
innovative, reliable solutions for our customers 
and partners.
Furthermore, I extend my deepest gratitude to the 
team at Route Mobile. Your contributions are the 
foundation of everything we have achieved and 
the driving force behind our future ambitions. You 
are the architects of our success, inspiring me daily 
with your dedication, expertise and passion. It is 
your commitment, drive, creativity and teamwork 
that turn challenges into milestones and ambitions 
into reality.
Thank you all for your continued trust and belief in 
our vision.
Mark James Reid

MD & CEO ’s Message
A Year of Strategic 
Transformation 
Unlocking Synergies with Proximus,  
BICS and Telesign
Our integration into the Proximus Global 
ecosystem is progressing with purpose. In the past 
year, we tapped into cost synergies by leveraging 
BICS’ global operator access and routing 
efficiencies. We have now laid the groundwork 
for the next phase of growth—cross-pollinating 
products and co-selling across markets through 
the combined sales force of Route, BICS 
and Telesign.
This collaboration enhances our credibility among 
large global enterprises and strengthens our direct 
enterprise access in previously underpenetrated 
markets such as Europe and North America. 
It also amplifies our ability to scale fraud protection, 
firewall services and digital identity offerings—capabilities that are increasingly sought after 
in a more regulated and privacy-conscious 
global environment.
Resilient Growth amid Industry Headwinds
Amid industry-wide changes, including evolving 
communication preferences and increasing 
scrutiny over artificial traffic, Route Mobile 
delivered a strong and stable performance.
Revenue from operations rose by 13.7% year-on-
year to ₹45,756 million, demonstrating resilience 
and adaptability. We processed 156 billion billable 
transactions during the year and maintained a net 
revenue retention rate of 107%—a strong indicator 
of platform strength and customer trust. While 
average realisations per transaction saw a marginal 
dip due to changes in traffic mix, our business 
fundamentals remained intact.It gives me great pleasure to 
present the Annual Report for 
FY 24–25—a year of meaningful 
transformation for Route 
Mobile. With our integration 
into Proximus Global and the 
combined strengths of BICS 
and Telesign, we have taken 
significant strides toward 
becoming a truly global player 
in the digital communications 
landscape. This strategic 
alignment has enhanced our 
ability to help enterprises and 
businesses connect, protect and 
engage with their customers 
securely and at scale.
Gross profit reached ₹9,509 
million, reflecting a 10.5% year-on-
year increase. Our EBITDA rose to 
₹5,278 million, up 3.3% year-on-year, 
underscoring operational efficiency 
and prudent financial management.
Notably, our new product revenues grew 38% 
year-on-year to ₹3,168 million, reflecting the rising 
enterprise demand for IP-based messaging—
WhatsApp, RCS and Email—as well as end-to-end 
omnichannel capabilities.
Advancing Up the Value Chain
FY 24–25 marked a pivotal chapter in our journey 
to become a solutions-led CPaaS provider. It was 
a year of purposeful transformation—where we 
made strategic strides to move up the value 
chain by building use-case driven innovations, 
forging meaningful partnerships and enhancing 
platform capabilities.
Our focus shifted beyond channels to solving 
real-world problems. We launched WhatsApp-
based ticketing solutions for metro systems in 
Jakarta, Hyderabad (L&T Metro) and Mumbai 
(Maha Mumbai Metro), enabling seamless urban 
transit experiences. We also supported IRCTC 
with WhatsApp-based utility communications 
and introduced RCS ticketing integrated with 
Google Wallet for commuters in Hyderabad. 
These initiatives reflect our transition from 
a channel provider to a trusted solutions 
enabler—enhancing both customer value and 
margin realisation.
We also launched 365guard, an AI-powered 
SMS spam and fraud protection solution that 
brings trust and safety to digital communication. 
Alongside, we secured strategic partnerships such 
as with PT MRT Jakarta and continued to expand 
our solutions portfolio across industries.
Our business milestones further validate this 
upward journey. We had the honour of hosting the 
Belgian Economic Mission, led by HRH Princess 
Astrid, reinforcing Indo-Belgian collaboration. 
Our leadership in RCS messaging earned us 
the ‘ Partnership Award’ at the RCS World Tour 
– India and we were recognised as a Platinum Winner for Conversational Commerce at the Telco 
Innovation Awards 2025. We also bagged two 
Exchange4media awards, were named Meta’s 
Solutions Partner of the Year 2024 – India and 
featured as a Niche Player in the Gartner Magic 
Quadrant for CPaaS 2024.
Shaping the Future: API Monetisation  
and Digital Identity
Looking forward, we see immense opportunity 
in telecom network API monetisation. With BICS’ 
network assets and Route’s enterprise customer 
access, we are well-positioned to develop and 
deploy new API layers tailored to enterprise use 
cases. Initiatives like Catana and Aduna signal a 
new era of programmable telco infrastructure and 
Route Mobile aims to play an active role in shaping 
this evolution.
At the same time, we are accelerating our 
investments in digital identity. While each 
geography presents unique regulatory and 
operator dynamics, we are working with 
stakeholders—including central banks and telecom 
operators—to deliver locally compliant solutions. 
With authentication shifting from SMS to more 
secure digital formats, digital identity will be central 
to how Route Mobile creates value going forward.
Committed to Innovation and  
Stakeholder Value
As we navigate a dynamic CPaaS environment, our 
focus remains steadfast: to be a global leader in 
trusted digital engagement. This means delivering 
innovative solutions, deepening customer 
relationships, expanding market reach and 
strengthening our platform. We will continue to 
build on our momentum, unlock synergies across 
Proximus Global and accelerate our presence in 
high-growth regions.
I extend my heartfelt gratitude to our employees 
for their commitment, our customers for their 
trust, our partners for their collaboration and 
you—our shareholders—for your continued 
support. Together, we are well-placed to lead 
the next wave of growth in secure, scalable and 
intelligent communications.
Thank you,
Rajdipkumar Gupta
Managing Director & CEO

Operating Context
Adapting to a 
Changing Ecosystem
Global CPaaS Market
Revenue and Forecast
The global CPaaS market is undergoing a pivotal 
evolution—from enabling transactional messaging 
to powering rich, omnichannel customer 
engagement. According to Juniper Research (April 
2025), the global CPaaS market is expected to 
generate over USD 34 billion in revenue by 2026, 
up 13% from USD 30 billion in 2025. By CY 2029, 
the total market value is projected to reach USD 48 
billion, marking a 60% increase from CY 2025.  
This growth is driven by several converging 
trends: rising enterprise demand for AI-driven 
personalisation, growing use of OTT messaging 
and RCS and the increasing importance of digital 
identity and fraud prevention solutions. CPaaS 
providers are transitioning from pure connectivity 
platforms to Customer Experience-as-a-Service 
(CXaaS) enablers—integrating communication 
APIs with CRM, CDPs, contact centres and 
marketing tools.
SMS, while still dominant in volume, is gradually 
yielding to richer formats. Identity APIs, 
Quality on Demand (QoD) and omnichannel 
orchestration are emerging as next-generation 
differentiators. Enterprises are investing in CPaaS 
to build intelligent, secure and scalable customer 
journeys across voice, messaging, email and 
digital touchpoints.
$48 Bn
Projected total market value 
by CY 2029*Rising Fraud and Regulatory 
Complexity
In CY 2024, CPaaS providers are navigating an 
increasingly complex regulatory landscape. 
Governments and telecom authorities across 
regions are tightening rules around data privacy, 
consent, cross-border messaging and platform 
accountability. Simultaneously, enterprises are 
under pressure to maintain compliance across 
multiple jurisdictions while protecting their users 
from evolving threats. The rise in fraud incidents 
has intensified the urgency for CPaaS players to 
embed regulatory readiness and advanced risk 
mitigation strategies across their platforms.Shifting Consumer Preferences  
and Channel Usage
Consumers today expect instant, always-on 
communication from brands and their preferences 
are shifting towards more intuitive, rich-media 
platforms. Conversational channels like WhatsApp, 
RCS and other OTT messengers are overtaking 
traditional SMS. This transformation is pushing 
enterprises to adopt omnichannel strategies that 
are real-time, hyper-personalised and natively 
embedded within users’ digital ecosystems. CPaaS 
players are responding by expanding support for 
interactive messaging, integrated chatbots and 
intelligent automation.
Digital Fraud and Artificially Inflated 
Traffic (AIT) Challenges
The volume and sophistication of digital fraud are 
rising sharply. In markets with high messaging 
throughput, such as India, artificially inflated traffic 
(AIT), spam, grey routes and phishing campaigns 
are eroding trust and inflating operational costs 
for businesses. CPaaS providers are increasingly 
investing in AI-powered detection systems, 
traffic filtering solutions and secure message 
delivery frameworks to counter these threats. 
There is a growing emphasis on building a trusted 
communication infrastructure that ensures 
message integrity, delivery transparency and end-
user protection.Outcome
 →Our omnichannel platform is enabled 
with required spam control features, 
including daily thresholds, approved 
templates, content validations and 
sender ID registrations, to control 
spam. This is available across most 
of the communication channels and 
the configurable controls facilitate 
communication over these channels.
 →Our communication platforms are cloud-
based and available to access from across 
the world, with data residency across 
most geographies. In this manner, data 
protection, data residency and such 
regulatory requirements are ensured.
 →Our Cloud-based and autoscalable 
platforms allow services to be delivered 
anywhere and at any scale. IP-based 
and firewall controls at the cloud level 
ensure access controls, in addition to 
authentication and 2FA controls.Outcome
 →Our platforms are designed to support 
end-to-end communications across various 
customer verticals, including e-commerce, 
BFSI, retail and hospitality. The customer 
experience of delivering traditional 
text messages to rich communications 
channels of OTT, as well as the interactive 
and conversational messaging 
requirements of any enterprise, can be 
addressed using the platform.
 →We provide API-based integrations 
across various channels, including 
SMS, WhatsApp, RCS, Viber, Email and 
Voice and support rich, interactive and 
conversational messaging experiences 
over these APIs for integrations. Enterprises 
can utilise these APIs for automating and 
enhancing interactive communications on 
their platforms.
 →Our omnichannel platform supports 
traditional SMS, as well as rich and 
interactive communication channels 
like WhatsApp, RCS and Viber, among 
others, serving as OTT channels within the 
platform. This is available through GUI and 
API based access. By doing so, we ensure 
a rich, interactive and conversational 
experience that meets the needs of 
the modern era of communications. 
Additionally, our team supports 
applications like OCEAN and Roubot, which 
use these channels to deliver campaign 
experiences, chatbot experiences and other 
interactive content.Outcome
 →Integration with Telecom operators 
worldwide enables us to provide telecom 
connectivity through SMS, Voice and RCS 
channels. The connectivity ensures that any 
enterprise communication requirement 
that requires messages to be delivered over 
these channels and across can be met. Our 
operator solutions like CPaaS in a Box and 
Operator RCS can help operators build the 
enterprise communications platform for 
any operator and offer it to their enterprise 
customers, seamlessly combining telecom 
channels (SMS, Voice, etc.) and OTT 
channels (WhatsApp, RCS, Viber, Email, 
etc.).

Our Strategic Priorities 
Redefining the CPaaS  
Value Chain
At Route Mobile, our strategic focus is on transforming enterprise 
communications by enabling smarter, more secure and seamless 
interactions across every digital touchpoint. Backed by the 
Proximus Global ecosystem—and strengthened by the combined 
expertise of BICS and Telesign—we are moving beyond basic 
connectivity to build an integrated application layer.
This evolution allows us to deliver intelligent, hyper-personalised and omnichannel 
engagement experiences that are scalable, compliant and globally relevant, unlocking 
new value for enterprises and redefining the future of digital communication.
To stay ahead in a rapidly evolving digital communications landscape, Route Mobile 
has defined four clear strategic priorities that align with our growth ambition and the 
opportunities unlocked by the Proximus Global ecosystem. Together, these priorities 
enable us to move up the value chain, expand globally and deliver smarter, more secure 
and scalable enterprise engagement solutions.
Moving Up the Value Chain
 We are advancing beyond basic connectivity 
into the application layer, creating higher-value 
solutions that integrate communication APIs 
with AI/ML, customer data platforms, mobile 
identity and marketing automation. Backed by 
the Proximus Global context, we are enabling 
enterprises to orchestrate intelligent, secure and 
hyper-personalised customer journeys at scale.Expanding Geographic Reach
By leveraging BICS’ global operator network 
and Telesign’s enterprise relationships, we are 
accelerating our expansion into mature markets 
such as Europe and the US, while strengthening 
our position in high-growth regions across 
Asia, Africa and the Middle East. This allows us 
to serve larger enterprises and tap into new 
opportunities worldwide.
Driving Product Innovation
Innovation is at the core of our strategy. We are 
focused on building solutions around digital 
fraud prevention, hyper-personalisation and 
integrated communications. From omnichannel 
orchestration to monetising WhatsApp Voice and 
eSIM capabilities, we are creating differentiated 
offerings that redefine enterprise communication 
and unlock new revenue streams.Unlocking Synergies Across Proximus Global
The integration with Proximus Global creates 
powerful synergies—a unified team, a consolidated 
product portfolio and enhanced cross-selling 
and upselling opportunities. It enables us to 
address global compliance requirements, 
streamline operations and deliver end-to-end 
communication, identity and security solutions that 
are globally scalable.Our Four Strategic PrioritiesMoving Up the Value Chain
Scaling the Application Layer for Smarter 
Engagement
We are evolving beyond basic connectivity 
to build an intelligent application layer 
that delivers higher value to enterprises. 
By integrating advanced technologies such 
as AI/ML, customer data platforms, mobile 
identity and marketing automation, we are 
enabling a shift from transactional messaging to 
experience-led engagement.
This transformation empowers enterprises to:
 →Orchestrate real-time, personalised and 
secure interactions
 →Seamlessly engage customers across SMS, Voice, 
RCS, WhatsApp and other digital channels
 →Enhance operational efficiency while 
strengthening trust and complianceWhy It Matters

Driving Product Innovation
Building Trusted, Personalised and 
Integrated Communication Experiences
Innovation lies at the heart of Route Mobile’s 
mission to deliver intelligent, secure and seamless 
enterprise engagement. As the digital landscape 
becomes more complex, we are reimagining 
communication beyond basic messaging—
bringing together trust, personalisation 
and interoperability to create richer, more 
meaningful interactions.
Our product strategy focuses on three core areas: 
digital trust and fraud prevention, AI-driven 
personalisation and omnichannel orchestration. 
These pillars not only enhance customer 
experience but also unlock new monetisation 
opportunities, positioning Route Mobile as a future-
ready leader in enterprise communications.
Building Digital Trust and Combating Fraud
With the growing sophistication of fraud and 
identity-related threats, secure communication 
has never been more critical. In collaboration with 
Telesign, we have advanced our capabilities in 
fraud mitigation and identity verification.Solutions like 365guard and AI-powered firewalls 
proactively detect spam, analyse behavioural risks 
and validate content in real time—protecting 
both users and enterprise brands from smishing, 
phishing and artificially inflated traffic (AIT). 
These capabilities form the foundation of a safer, 
more trusted communication ecosystem that 
strengthens enterprise resilience in an increasingly 
regulated environment.
AI-Powered Personalisation: Elevating CX
Today’s consumers expect brands to speak to 
them in the right context, at the right time. 
To meet this demand, we are embedding 
AI-driven personalisation into our platform, 
enabling enterprises to tailor interactions based 
on user behaviour, location, device type and 
language preferences.
Whether it is a geo-targeted promotion for a retail 
customer or a transaction alert tailored to an 
individual’s identity profile in financial services, 
our platform allows businesses to deliver hyper-
personalised experiences at scale, increasing 
relevance, trust and engagement.Our Strategic Priorities 
Orchestrating Omnichannel  
Journeys Seamlessly
Customers today move fluidly across multiple 
touchpoints—SMS, WhatsApp, RCS, Email, 
Voice and beyond. Our OCEAN (Omnichannel 
Experience Automation Network) simplifies this 
complexity by bringing all channels into a single, 
orchestrated flow.
This ensures:
 →Consistency of brand voice and messaging
 →Compliance and channel intelligence
 →Smarter journeys that meet customers where 
they are, on their preferred platform
With OCEAN, enterprises can easily create, manage 
and optimise marketing, service and transactional 
campaigns, delivering a truly connected experience 
across every interaction.
Unlocking New Monetisation Avenues
Innovation at Route Mobile is not limited to 
improving customer experience—it is also opening 
high-margin monetisation opportunities.
 →WhatsApp Voice is expanding conversational 
commerce and enabling enterprises to elevate 
customer service beyond text. →eSIM Solutions are redefining global connectivity 
for industries like travel, logistics and fintech.
 →RCS Business Messaging is transforming plain 
SMS into visually rich, interactive conversations, 
enhancing engagement while enabling new 
revenue streams.
From CPaaS to CXaaS: The Future of 
Enterprise Engagement
Each innovation brings us closer to becoming 
a full-stack Customer Experience-as-a-Service 
(CXaaS) provider. By combining communications, 
identity, personalisation and analytics, we empower 
enterprises to move faster, connect smarter and 
serve better in an always-on, digital-first world.
The Innovation Impact
 →Safer digital interactions
 →Hyper-personalised customer journeys
 →Unified omnichannel orchestration
 →New revenue and engagement models

Expanding Geographic Reach
Strengthening Our Global Footprint 
Through Proximus Global
Our integration with the Proximus Group, 
encompassing BICS and Telesign, marks a pivotal 
moment in our journey from a regional CPaaS 
provider to a truly global digital communications 
player. This partnership amplifies our international 
growth strategy, combining Route Mobile’s 
enterprise-focused innovation with Proximus’s 
global scale and infrastructure.
This expanded ecosystem positions us to 
strengthen our presence in mature markets such 
as Europe and North America, while continuing 
to scale rapidly in high-growth regions including 
India, Africa and Asia-Pacific. By tapping into 
BICS’ deep operator access and Telesign’s 
enterprise networks, we are unlocking broader 
market penetration and a wider product reach 
across continents.
From Regional Strength to Global Leadership
What began as a strategic integration between 
Route Mobile and Telesign has now evolved 
into the larger Proximus Global framework, 
with BICS adding unmatched global operator 
connectivity. This evolution reflects our broader 
ambition—to lead the next wave of digital 
engagement worldwide.
Through this integration, we are combining:
 →BICS’ carrier-facing strengths and global 
telecom access
 →Telesign’s expertise in digital identity and 
fraud prevention
 →Route Mobile’s enterprise innovation and 
omnichannel orchestration
This creates a comprehensive ecosystem capable 
of delivering intelligent, secure and scalable 
communications for enterprises, no matter where 
they operate.Unlocking Future-ready Monetisation
Global expansion is not just about reach; it’s also 
about creating future-ready revenue models. 
Initiatives such as Catana and Aduna are at the 
forefront of this strategy, enabling telecom network 
API monetisation.
By blending BICS’ operator-level access with 
Route Mobile’s enterprise-layer capabilities, we 
are opening new monetisation pathways for 
fraud prevention, authentication, geolocation and 
advanced network-driven services.
Leading the Next Wave of Channel Innovation
As we prioritise IP-based communication channels 
such as WhatsApp, RCS and in-app messaging, 
our goal is to lead the next evolution of enterprise 
engagement. By offering intelligent, secure and 
integrated solutions at scale, we are helping 
businesses transition from traditional SMS-
based communication to richer, more interactive 
customer experiences across markets.

Scope 2

Scope 3
Collaborating with suppliers and partners to 
optimise transportation and logistics, reducing 
emissions across our value chain.

Human Capital
Our People, Our Advantage
People power our journey at Route Mobile. By prioritising 
diversity, learning and engagement, we create an environment 
where talent is nurtured, collaboration is strengthened and every 
employee feels valued and equipped to grow with us.
Diversity, Equity & Inclusion: A Culture of 
Belonging
At Route Mobile, diversity, equity and inclusion 
(DEI) are not just values—they are essential pillars 
of our culture. We believe that diverse teams and 
inclusive environments fuel innovation, enhance 
collaboration and drive sustainable growth.
We have embedded DEI into our systems, policies 
and leadership practices to ensure every employee 
feels seen, heard and empowered.
Cultivating Inclusive Leadership
We equip leaders with the tools to build and 
nurture diverse teams through mandatory Inclusive 
Leadership Workshops. These sessions help foster 
empathy, eliminate unconscious bias and promote 
equitable leadership practices.
Empowering Communities Within
Our Employee Resource Groups (ERGs) and 
mentorship circles create safe spaces for dialogue, 
support and personal growth, strengthening the 
sense of belonging across the organisation.
Zero Tolerance for Harassment
A respectful workplace is non-negotiable. We have 
implemented robust anti-harassment policies, 
supported by mandatory training programmes 
to ensure awareness, accountability and a culture 
of respect at every level.Shaping Future Leaders
Through structured mentorship programmes and 
leadership development tracks, we actively identify 
and nurture high-potential talent. These initiatives 
are designed to cultivate future-ready leaders who 
can inspire teams, drive innovation and lead Route 
Mobile into its next phase of growth.
Employee Engagement:  
Connecting through Purpose
Employee engagement at Route Mobile goes 
beyond activities and programmes—it is a 
philosophy rooted in trust, recognition and shared 
purpose. We design initiatives that reflect our core 
values while addressing the diverse aspirations of 
our people.
Rewards and Recognition Programmes
We celebrate the achievements of our people 
through structured rewards and recognition 
programmes, designed to motivate, appreciate and 
reinforce high performance across the organisation.
Driving Transparent Communication
Regular Town Hall meetings, internal newsletters 
and leadership updates ensure open dialogue, 
clarity and alignment—building mutual trust and 
keeping teams connected to our mission.
Building Collaboration through Team Spirit
We regularly host team-building activities 
across departments to foster camaraderie, 
enhance morale and encourage collaboration, 
strengthening our unified culture.

Communities
Building Stronger, 
Resilient Communities
At Route Mobile, we believe that businesses thrive when communities 
flourish. Guided by this philosophy, our community initiatives focus on 
education, healthcare, sports and welfare, ensuring meaningful impact 
and sustainable value creation. Through strategic partnerships and 
targeted programmes, we address local needs while fostering inclusion, 
dignity and long-term growth.
Advancing Access to Education
Education is one of the most powerful tools 
for social transformation. We are committed 
to making quality education accessible to 
underserved communities, bridging learning gaps 
and empowering students to unlock their potential.
In collaboration with the Sai Prabodhan Trust, 
we supported initiatives that provide learning 
resources, skill-building opportunities and 
mentorship to students from marginalised 
backgrounds, reinforcing our belief in the 
transformative power of education.
Promoting Healthcare and Senior Care
Caring for the most vulnerable, particularly senior 
citizens, remains a key pillar of our CSR efforts. 
We partnered with the Habitat for Humanity 
Foundation to contribute to the construction of 
an old-age home in Bandra, Mumbai, designed to 
offer dignity, comfort and compassionate care for 
the elderly.
Fostering Inclusion through Sports
We view sports as a powerful catalyst for social 
inclusion, community bonding and holistic 
development. Through targeted grants,  Route Mobile extended support to rural, 
Paralympic and Olympic sports, nurturing talent 
and expanding access to sporting opportunities for 
all, regardless of background or ability.
By supporting diverse sporting disciplines, we aim 
to inspire individuals, foster resilience and promote 
equality in the sporting ecosystem.
Driving Community Welfare
Beyond education, healthcare and sports, Route 
Mobile is deeply engaged in community welfare 
initiatives that respond to local needs. Through 
tailored grant programmes and collaborative 
partnerships, we focus on creating long-term, 
sustainable value that uplifts communities and 
fosters inclusive regional growth.
Building Stronger, Resilient Communities

commitment to inclusive growth and the

Board’s Report
Dear Members of Route Mobile,
Your directors are pleased to present the Twenty First 
Annual Report of Route Mobile Limited (‘Company’/ 
‘RML’/ ‘Route Mobile’) along with the audited financial 
statements (consolidated as well as standalone) 
for the financial year ended March 31, 2025. This 
Board’s Report is prepared based on the standalone 
financial statements of the Company for the year 
under review and also presents the key highlights of 
performance of subsidiaries, and their contribution 
to the overall performance of the Company during 
the year under review.
1. Corporate Overview
Route Mobile is a leading CPaaS provider 
that caters to enterprises, over-the-top (OTT) 
players, and mobile network operators (MNO). 
Established in 2004, we are serving more than 
40,000 customers worldwide. Our goal is to 
add value at multiple touchpoints across the 
Omnichannel CXPaaS value chain while building 
on unique industry use cases for our clients. We 
are headquartered in Mumbai, India, and have operations in over 20 countries throughout 
Asia Pacific, the Middle East, Africa, Europe, 
and the Americas. The Company is listed on the 
Indian bourses BSE Limited (“BSE”) (BSE Scrip 
Code: 543228) & National Stock Exchange of 
India Limited (“NSE”) (NSE Symbol: ROUTE). 
Pursuant to the amendment to Regulation 3(2) 
of the SEBI (Listing Obligations and Disclosures 
Requirements) Regulation, 2015 (“SEBI Listing 
Regulations”), with effect from December 31, 
2024, recognised stock exchanges must annually 
rank listed entities based on their average 
market capitalisation from July 1 to December 
31. The Company’s average market cap during 
this period ranks it among India’s top 1,000 
listed companies. (Ranks 543 and 545 as of 
December 31, 2024. Source: NSE and BSE).
2. State of the Affairs of the Company
The performance of our omni-channel 
communication & other businesses are 
detailed out in the Management Discussion 
and Analysis Report, which forms part of the 
Annual Report.

and Note on Financial and Operations
RML offers a scalable and flexible Omnichannel 
CPaaS platform to enterprises across industry 
verticals, globally. Details of your Company’s 
annual financial performance for the financial 
year ended March 31, 2025, as published on the 
Company’s website and presented during the 
Analyst Meet after declaration of annual results, 
can be accessed at https://routemobile.com/wp-
content/uploads/2025/05/Outcome-of-Board-
meeting-May-7-2025.pdf . With strong industry 
tailwinds, the Global CPaaS market is expected 
to grow to US$48bn by 2029 (12% CAGR over 
2025-2029).
During the reporting period, the Company 
posted a consolidated revenue of H 45,756 mn, 
a 34% revenue CAGR FY 2021- FY 2025. During 
the reporting period, Company’s total income 
on a consolidated basis increased by 14% Y-o-Y 
and PAT declined by 14%. On a standalone 
basis, total income increased by 21% though 
operational efficiencies and PAT went up by 4%.
We processed 156 bn billable transactions in 
FY 2024-25 and our net revenue retention in 
FY 2024-25 was 107%.
5. Key Service Milestones
Hyderabad Metro: Route Mobile has enabled 
a ticket booking experience through RCS 
(Rich Communications Service), allowing 
commuters to use RCS on their Android 
devices to book train tickets. If the device is not RCS-capable, the platform will automatically 
switch the booking experience to WhatsApp 
for that user. This is a unique and first-of-its-
kind experience that covers an RCS-based 
user-initiated journey for ticket booking, with 
a WhatsApp fallback.
Samsung India: Route Mobile has enabled 
Samsung with WhatsApp-based campaigns, 
lead generation and customer service 
across India. 
Optimised marketing messaging delivery in 
WhatsApp through MM-Lite:  Route Mobile 
has provided optimised marketing messaging 
delivery to 30% of our customers to enhance 
customer experience and business conversions 
for enterprises. 
Landmark group: Route Mobile has 
implemented WhatsApp-based logistics 
services in the UAE, including tracking and 
last-mile delivery optimisations. An interactive, 
chatbot-driven conversational experience was 
deployed to enhance the user experience for 
end customers and improve business delivery.
Americana group: Route Mobile has enabled 
WhatsApp-based QSR service conversational 
experience for order booking, delivery tracking 
and feedback management across 4 brands 
and multiple countries across the ME region.
Search to RCS: Route Mobile has implemented 
a significant milestone in delivering 'Search 
to RCS', enabling users to search on Google 
and connect with brands through RCS-
based conversations.

10. Dividend
Your Directors have recommended a final 
dividend of H 2/- (Rupees Two) per equity 
share of H 10/- (Rupees Ten) each (20%) for the 
financial year ended March 31, 2025, subject to shareholders’ approval at the ensuing annual 
general meeting of the Company. The total 
dividend for the financial year ended March 
31, 2025, aggregates to H 11/- (Rupees Eleven 
only) per equity share of the face value of 
H 10/- (Rupees Ten) each, including the interim 
dividend of H 9/- (Rupees Nine), per equity share 
as approved by the Board of Directors at their 
meeting held on October 21, 2024 and January 
28, 2025 respectively, which was paid thereafter. 
The dividend recommended is in line with the 
Dividend Distribution Policy of the Company. 
The Dividend Distribution Policy, in terms of 
Regulation 43A of SEBI Listing Regulations is 
available on the Company’s website at https://
routemobile.com/investors/corporate-policies/ . 
Pursuant to the Finance Act, 2020, dividend 
income is taxable in the hands of the 
shareholders w.e.f. April 01, 2020 and the 
Company is required to deduct tax at source 
(TDS) on dividend paid to the Members at 
prescribed rates as per the Income-tax Act, 
1961. For more details in this regard, please 
refer to the ‘Notes’ section of the Notice to the 
Annual General Meeting (“AGM”).

13.  Highlights of Performance of Subsidiary 
Companies
Your Company along with its subsidiaries 
provide a wide range of cloud communication 
platform services to enterprises, OTT players 
and detection and traffic analytics, monitoring 
traffic and administration of SMS Firewall and 
a comprehensive 24/7 Managed Service and 
customer support solutions, back office & 
consultancy services.
In accordance with Section 136 of the 
Companies Act, 2013 (“the Act”), the 
audited financial statements, including the 
consolidated financial statements and related 
information of the Company and accounts (as per local law requirement) of each of its 
subsidiaries, are available on our website 
at www.routemobile.com . A short description of 
business and performance of major subsidiaries 
are provided below:
Route Mobile (UK) Limited (‘RML UK’): RML UK 
is engaged in the business of inter alia  providing 
technology services for mobile communications 
with a focus on messaging. The Standalone 
Gross Revenue of RML UK for FY 2024-25 
stood at GBP 223,483,409 (Previous Year: GBP 
183,383,463) and the Profit after Tax stood at 
GBP 8,711,414 (Previous Year: GBP 9,040,549).
During the year under review, RML UK has 
acquired 7,507,500 shares of IDR 1000/- 
aggregating to IDR 7,507,500,000 of PT Route 
Mobile Indonesia (“RM Indonesia”) on November 
22, 2024 to meet the local law requirements 
of Indonesian Investment Law for a minimum 
paid-up capital of IDR 10,000,000,000 for 
foreign-owned companies (PMA) registered in 
the country. Upon allotment of equity shares by 
RM Indonesia, the total investment of RML UK in 
RM Indonesia is 99,99,990 shares, representing 
99.90% of the total equity share capital in 
RM Indonesia.
Routesms Solutions FZE (‘Routesms FZE’): 
Routesms FZE is engaged in the business of inter 
alia providing technology services for mobile 
communications with a focus on messaging and 
voice solutions. The Revenue of the company for 
FY 2025 stood at AED 130,012,006 (Previous 
Year: 165,841,210). Routesms FZE earned net 
profit for the year of AED 32,441,559 (Previous 
Year: AED 52,855,232).
Masivian S.A.S. (‘Masivian’): Masivian is a leading 
cloud communications platform service provider 
in Latin America, with leadership position in 
Colombia and Peru, as well as a presence in several 
countries in the region. Masivian’s differentiated 
and well-adapted cloud communication platform, 
security & data analytics offer a comprehensive 
suite of communications, marketing, and unique 
AI-powered products for enterprises. Masivian 
offers multichannel notification services through 
SMS, Email, as well as Voice, serving marquee 
clients across Colombia & Peru. For the year ended 
March 31, 2025, Masivian clocked Consolidated 
Revenue of COP 1,32,70,57,10,136 (Previous Year: 
Revenue of COP 120,603,837,915) and Profit after 
tax of COP 10,25,44,70,183 (Previous Year: Profit 
after tax COP 10,307,606,404).365squared Ltd. (‘365squared’): 365squared is 
engaged in the business of inter alia  providing 
technology services for mobile communications 
with a focus on SMS filtering, analytics and 
monetisation. 365analytics is a real time detection 
and traffic analytics software with an intelligence 
that is updated constantly based on our global 
intelligence. The Revenue of 365squared for 
FY 2024-25 stood at EURO 9,742,924 (Previous 
Year: EURO 8,557,662). 365squared earned a 
Profit after tax of EURO 2,363,878 (Previous Year: 
EURO 482,615).
M.R Messaging FZE(“MRM”): MRM is involved 
in the business of CPaaS solutions including 
A2P messaging, number lookup and 2-way 
messaging, offered to enterprises as well as 
aggregators, using its technology platform. 
Audited Revenue of the Consolidated MRM for 
the financial year ended March 31, 2025 was AED 
330,969,316 (Previous Year: AED 315,220,156) and 
Profit after tax for the period was AED 15,126,078 
(Previous Year: AED 26,499,722).
Route Mobile Communication Services 
Company (fka “Interteleco International 
for Modern Communication Services”) 
(“RMC”): RMC is engaged in the business 
of communication services ( viz. messaging 
solutions / SMS business) in Kuwait. For the year 
ended March 31, 2025, RMC generated a revenue 
of KWD 2,264,039 (Previous Year: KWD 2,999,277) 
with a profit of KWD 2,95,444 (Previous Year: 
K WD 5,67,549).

21. Human Capital
At the heart of our success lies our people our 
greatest strength. They shape our culture, drive 
our performance, and help us build a workplace 
that supports both professional and personal 
growth. We are committed to fostering a 
positive, engaging environment where every 
employee feels valued and empowered.
To continuously enhance the employee 
experience, we leverage multiple feedback 
channels, including skip-level meetings, 
leadership-driven town halls, and exit interviews. 
These mechanisms help us identify areas for 
improvement and strengthen our position as an 
employer of choice.
We take pride in promoting diversity and 
inclusivity. With a workforce spread across 
21 countries, our diverse talent pool brings 
together a wide range of perspectives and 
experiences. Despite geographical distances, 
our employees share and embody a unified 
Route Culture — one that values collaboration, 
respect, and excellence.Employee communication and engagement are 
central to our people strategy. Our quarterly town 
halls keep teams informed about organisational 
updates, while meet-and-greet sessions with 
leadership for new joiners ensure meaningful 
connections from day one. Milestones and 
key achievements are celebrated across all 
offices, reinforcing a sense of community 
and accomplishment.
Our strong Rewards and Recognition programs 
ensure that exceptional performance does 
not go unnoticed. Quarterly recognition 
events spotlight outstanding contributors, 
and our Annual Recognition Night honour’s 
those who have consistently demonstrated 
excellence throughout the year. We also 
take this opportunity to celebrate long-term 
commitment, recognising employees who have 
completed 5, 10, and 15 years with us. Together, 
we are building a workplace defined by passion, 
purpose, and pride.

23.  Board Diversity and Policy on Director’s 
Appointment and Remuneration
The responsibilities of the board of directors 
have been on the corporate agenda for years. 
Acting as the agents of shareholders, directors 
are expected collectively to devise operational 
and financial strategies for the organisation 
and to monitor the effectiveness of the 
company’s practices. Directors are responsible, 
for devising strategies through critical analysis 
and effective problem solving. One of the pitfalls 
behind the decision-making process in the 
boardroom is ‘groupthink’, which is described 
as a psychological behavior of minimising 
conflicts and reaching a consensus decision 
without critically evaluating alternative ideas 
in a cohesive in-group environment. Diversity 
in boardrooms creates an impact outside the 
boardroom, too. It can attract more top talent 
and create greater employee satisfaction. The 
labor market increasingly evaluates companies 
on whether they maintain an inclusive and 
diverse environment. Companies that excel in 
this area, particularly in the boardroom, achieve 
a competitive advantage by winning the war for 
talent. Your Company has been mindful of this 
and have always strived for relevant diversity 
in the Board representing a healthy mix of 
gender & experience. The policy on ‘Nomination 
and Remuneration’ and ‘Board Diversity’ 
adopted by the Board sets out the criteria for 
determining qualifications, positive attributes 
and independence while evaluating a person for 
appointment / reappointment as a Director or as 
KMP, with no discrimination on the grounds of 
gender, race or ethnicity, nationality or country of 
origin. The Board Diversity policy is available on 
the Company’s website at https://routemobile.
com/investors/corporate-policies/ .

27.  Internal Financial Controls, their 
adequacy and Internal Auditors
Internal Control are often an area of focus for 
investors, creditors, shareholders and Board 
members, among other stakeholders, when 
ensuring that the organisation provides accurate 
financial reporting which shows its state of 
operations in today’s constantly changing 
business environment. At Route Mobile, Internal 
financial controls are the policies and procedures 
adopted by the company for ensuring the 
orderly and efficient conduct of its business, 
including adherence to the company’s policies, 
the safeguarding of its assets, the prevention 
and detection of frauds and errors, the accuracy 
and completeness of the records. Section 134(5)
(e) of the Act requires, the Board of every listed 
Company to lay down Internal Financial Control 
Policy to be followed by the Company which 
helps in ensuring the orderly and efficient 
conduct of its business, including adherence 
to Company’s policies, the safeguarding of its assets, the prevention and detection of frauds 
and errors, the accuracy and completeness of 
the Accounting records and timely preparation 
of reliable financial information. As per section 
177 (4) (vii) of the Act, the Audit Committee 
require to evaluate the Internal Financial Control 
("IFC") of the Company. At Route Mobile, IFC 
has been designed to mitigate Operational 
risks, including segregation of duties, checks 
and balances, protection of company’s funds, 
operating systems, management information 
systems, management reporting, front and 
back office operations, contingency planning 
and disaster recovery.
Walker Chandiok & Co LLP, the statutory auditors 
of RML have audited the financial statements 
included in this annual report and have issued 
an attestation report on the company’s internal 
control over financial reporting (as defined in 
Section 143 of the Act). RML has appointed 
M/s Pipalia Singhal & Associates to carry out 
internal audit of its activities for FY 2024-25. The 
audit is based on an internal audit plan, which 
is reviewed each year in consultation with the 
statutory auditors. RML also undergoes periodic 
audit by specialised third party consultants and 
professionals for business specific compliances 
such as quality management, service 
management, information security, etc. Based 
on its evaluation (as defined in Section 177 of Act 
and Regulation 18 of SEBI Listing Regulations), 
audit committee has noted that, as of March 31, 
2025, the company’s internal financial controls 
were adequate and operating effectively.

30. Risk Management
At Route Mobile, we view effective risk 
management as essential to our ability to create 
long-term value and achieve sustainable growth. 
Risk management is not a standalone function, 
but an integral part of our strategy, culture, 
and day-to-day operations. It helps us navigate 
uncertainties, respond proactively to emerging 
challenges, and make informed decisions in a 
fast-evolving business environment.
We have implemented a robust, enterprise-
wide Risk Management Framework aligned 
with international standards. This framework 
provides a structured and systematic approach to the identification, analysis, evaluation, 
treatment, monitoring, and review of risks across 
the organisation. It encompasses a broad range 
of risk categories including strategic, financial, 
operational, compliance, representational, and 
environmental, social, and governance (ESG) 
risks. In accordance with Section 134(3)(n) of the 
Act, the Board takes overall responsibility for the 
oversight of the Company’s risk management 
framework and ensures that appropriate 
systems are in place to identify and manage 
risks effectively.
The Risk Management Committee of the 
Board, chaired by an Independent Director, 
is responsible for reviewing and guiding the 
implementation of the Risk Management 
Framework and monitoring the effectiveness 
of risk management practices. It conducts 
regular reviews of key risks, mitigation plans, 
and emerging issues to ensure alignment 
with the Company’s strategic objectives. Risk 
management initiatives are driven from the 
top by the Risk Management Committee and 
are cascaded across the organisation through 
functional leadership. Functional heads play 
an active role in implementing risk controls 
and embedding risk awareness in day-to-day 
decision-making within their respective areas.
For each key risk identified, appropriate response 
strategies are developed and implemented to 
ensure that risks are effectively mitigated or 
managed. These strategies are integrated into 
operational plans and are regularly reviewed 
to reflect changes in the internal and external 
business environment.
The Risk Management Framework is further 
supported by internal audit mechanisms, which 
provide independent assurance on the design 
and effectiveness of risk controls. In addition, 
Route Mobile promotes active communication 
and consultation across all levels to ensure 
early identification of risks and an informed, 
collaborative approach to risk management. 
Further details regarding the composition 
of the Risk Management Committee and 
meetings held during the financial year are 
provided in the Corporate Governance section 
of this Annual Report, in accordance with the 
applicable provisions of the Act, and SEBI 
Listing Regulations.
Risk management is an ongoing process 
aimed at identifying, analysing, evaluating, 
and addressing potential loss exposures. 
This process includes monitoring risk control measures and financial resources to mitigate 
the adverse effects of loss, which can stem from 
various sources:
 • Financial Risks:  Costs of claims and 
liability judgements.
 • Operational Risks: Labor strikes and other 
operational disruptions.
 • Perimeter Risks: External factors such as 
weather or political changes.
 • Strategic Risks: Management changes or 
loss of reputation.
At its core, business and investing involve 
allocating resources and capital to chosen risks. 
In an environment of uncertainty, organisations 
may take steps to avoid certain risks, pursue 
high-reward risks, and measure and mitigate 
their exposure as necessary. Effective risk 
management processes and tools simplify 
complex business and financial challenges in 
uncertain conditions.
Global corporations closely monitor how 
large companies manage and respond to risk 
failures to avoid similar mistakes. They often 
underestimate both the internal and external 
costs of risk failures, including the significant 
time managers need for damage control. As 
corporate governance demands increase, 
organisations must focus on their overarching 
goal: creating optimal value for customers 
and shareholders.
Ultimately, risk management in corporate 
governance is about implementing principles 
that guide companies toward strategic, 
profitable risks while avoiding excessive 
risk taking.
At Route Mobile, risk management initiatives are 
driven from the top by the Risk Management 
Committee and disseminated to each functional 
head. These initiatives focus on critical areas such 
as finance, internal financial control, system and 
network security, and data privacy.
 • System and Data Security:  Quarterly 
updates on system and data security 
upgrades are provided to the Risk 
Management Committee.
 • Internal Audits:  Regular security audits 
including vulnerability and penetration 
tests are executed by CERT-In empaneled 
auditors. Additionally, regular third party 
audits are also conducted by and for 
BFSI customers. • Compliance:  The Board has entrusted each 
functional head with ensuring compliance 
with all relevant laws. Each functional 
head submits a compliance certificate to 
the Compliance Officer, who then present 
these to the Board quarterly.
The Risk Management Policy is available on the 
company's website at https://routemobile.com/
investors/corporate-policies/ . 
31. Cyber Security/Information Security
Over the past year, we have focused on 
continuously enhancing and strengthening our 
cybersecurity systems. Leveraging both internal 
resources and external partnerships, we have 
aimed to maintain robust security measures and 
improve our overall system performance.
The Company continues to face various 
cybersecurity risks through both direct and 
supply chain interactions. These risks include 
intentional and hostile actions, accidental 
breaches, and negligence. The primary 
concern remains system intrusions leading to 
data leakage. To address these risks, our Risk 
Management Committee has deliberated on 
several key areas:
 • Enhanced Security Policies:  We have 
strengthened our security policies by 
implementing mandatory measures such as 
IP whitelisting, Two-Factor Authentication 
(2FA), and Dark Web monitoring to 
proactively track potential Indicators of 
Compromise (IOCs). 
 • ISO 27001 Surveillance: Ongoing 
surveillance and adherence to ISO 27001 
standards remain a priority. 
 • Comprehensive Vulnerability Assessment 
and Penetration Testing:  Regular 
Vulnerability Assessment and Penetration 
Testing (VAPT) for RMLAPIs are conducted to 
identify and rectify security gaps.
We continue to implement and enhance our role-
based access controls, ensuring that employees 
have appropriate access levels according to their 
roles and responsibilities. Additionally, we have 
further streamlined our internal IT practices to 
provide rapid support to internal teams, which is 
critical for threat identification and response, as 
well as overall business productivity. 
Our commitment to cybersecurity includes 
continuously evaluating the latest tools and 
services to strengthen and expedite our 
Route Mobile    |   Annual Report 2024-25
60 61
Corporate Overview | Performance | Strategic Overview | Sustainability Performance | Statutory Reports  | Financial Statements
=== page 34 ===
detection and response systems. This proactive 
approach helps us stay ahead of emerging 
threats and ensures that our cybersecurity 
infrastructure remains robust and effective. 
In line with evolving data privacy regulations, we 
have updated our data processing agreements 
to ensure compliance with major data privacy 
laws globally. These updates cover GDPR, CCPA, 
and other significant data privacy regulations, 
ensuring that we meet the highest standards of 
data protection and privacy. 
We have taken a firm stance on our security 
policies with both customers and partners, 
ensuring that our security requirements are 
clearly communicated and enforced. This 
includes stringent measures for data handling, 
access control, and compliance with our security 
protocols to mitigate risks across the board. 
We have launched several initiatives to enhance 
cybersecurity awareness and training among 
employees. These include regular training 
sessions, phishing simulation exercises, and 
updated protocols for handling sensitive 
information. Our goal is to foster a culture of 
security mindfulness across the organisation. 
Looking ahead, we plan to further invest in 
advanced cybersecurity technologies such 
as AI-driven threat detection and response 
systems. Additionally, we aim to enhance our 
incident response capabilities and expand our 
cybersecurity team to address the growing 
complexity of cyber threats. 
By maintaining a proactive and adaptive 
cybersecurity strategy, we are committed 
to safeguarding our Company’s assets and 
ensuring the integrity of our operations.

on Vigil Mechanism and Whistle Blower in

the Act and Regulation 22 of the SEBI Listing 
Regulations for receiving and redressing complaints from employees, directors and other

behaviour, actual or suspected fraud.

cases. The Whistleblower (Vigil Mechanism)

of sexual harassment at workplace in line with 
the provisions of the Sexual Harassment of 
Women at Workplace (Prevention, Prohibition 
and Redressal) Act, 2013 and Rules framed 
thereunder. Internal Complaints Committee 
(‘ICC’) is in place for all works and offices of 
the Company to redress complaints received 
regarding sexual harassment.

of Women at Workplace (Prevention, Prohibition 
and Redressal) Act, 2013. Furthermore, there was

as well as ending of the financial year and no

from Dhrumil M Shah & Co. LLP, Practicing
